## 1. Dual-Swarm Adversarial Review Methodology & Round 1 Summary

Two parallel, isolated review swarms spanning three model families audited all specifications and research documents:
1. **NTM 4-Pane Isolated-Context Swarm:** `OPUS` (`claude-opus-5-5@high`), `FABLE` (`claude-fable-5-2@high`), `SOL` (`gpt-6.2-sol@high`), and `ASTRA` (`gpt-6.2-astra@high`).
2. **Sub-Agent Adversarial Swarm:** `NEON` (`gemini-4-neon-xhigh`) and `CARBON` (`gemini-4-carbon-high`).

## 3. Complete Round 1 Disposition & Traceability Table (`104` Findings — Verified Closed)

### 3.1 `OPUS` Round 1 (`OPUS-R1-01` .. `OPUS-R1-24`)

| ID | Sev | Finding Summary | Disposition & Canonical Remediation | Remediated Artifacts |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `OPUS-R1-01` | Critical | Host `os-sandbox` lacked default-deny writes and `<workspace>` root validation. | **Accepted (Closed).** Default-deny file writes, RO `<workspace>` in `--mode plan`, and `exit 64` split unsafe-root check. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` B0/D2, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R1-02` | Critical | Missing Mach/XPC, non-bus UDS, and D-Bus confinement allowed host IPC escape. | **Accepted (Closed).** `(deny mach-lookup)` with canonical 10-service allowlist **never** allowing `SecurityServer`/`securityd*`, `launchservicesd`, `appleevents`, `com.apple.pasteboard.1`, or `xpc.launchd`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` B0, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R1-03` | Critical | Case-sensitive diff-promotion denylist bypassed on case-insensitive APFS (`.Git/hooks`). | **Accepted (Closed).** `_PC_CASE_SENSITIVE` detection + Unicode NFC + full case-folding + `is_hfs_dotgit` (`exit 77`). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` B4/F6, `research/02` §4 |
| `OPUS-R1-04` | High | Host `os-sandbox` protected-path list was narrower than VM Diff Gate. | **Accepted (Closed).** Single normative B4 Protected Path Set shared identically across host `os-sandbox` and VM Diff Gate. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` B0/B4, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R1-05` | High | Terminal `O_NOFOLLOW` missed intermediate symlinks and concurrent host writers. | **Accepted (Closed).** Component-wise `openat(..., O_NOFOLLOW_ANY)` / `openat2(RESOLVE_NO_SYMLINKS\|RESOLVE_BENEATH)` under `~/.local/share/acli/workspace-locks/project-<proj_hash>.owner`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` B4/F6, `research/02` §4 |
| `OPUS-R1-06` | High | `seccomp(deny AF_VSOCK)` bypassed by `io_uring`/compat ABIs; `AF_VSOCK` ignores netns. | **Accepted (Closed).** Native `AUDIT_ARCH_*` check + `io_uring_*` block + `kernel.io_uring_disabled=2` + boot-latched `vsock:5040`/`5102` + `ACLI_BUS_TOKEN`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md`, `design.md` B2 |
| `OPUS-R1-07` | High | Shared in-guest `GMCP` (`serena`) bridged per-pane `bwrap` namespaces. | **Accepted (Closed).** Lazy on-demand `GMCP` inside each pane's own `bwrap` + cgroup + seccomp. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` D1, `research/02` §3 |
| `OPUS-R1-08` | High | FQDN-only `api.github.com` token injection bypassed Zone-4 mutating GitHub MCP deny. | **Accepted (Closed).** RFC 3986 `(provider, fqdn, method, path_prefix)` allowlists + host-internal Copilot `gho_` exchange. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` B3, `research/02` §4 |
| `OPUS-R1-09` | High | Sandbox CLI shared controlling TTY with parent shell (`TIOCSTI` injection). | **Accepted (Closed).** Zero-host-TTY fd invariant outside interactive PTY relay + SBPL `/dev/tty*` read/write/ioctl deny + Linux `--dev /dev`. | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/01` §1.3 |
| `OPUS-R1-10` | High | Binary pin ladder trusted unauthenticated cache metadata and executed by mutable path. | **Accepted (Closed).** `ACLISIG1` Ed25519 `ACLIIDX1` + file-and-dir `T0` manifest cache + `T2` `SHA-256(CodeDirectory)` + direct `node` exec + Linux `fexecve`. | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `design.md` D5, `research/01` §5 |
| `OPUS-R1-11` | Medium | Forgeable bypass env var could silently disable sandboxing for child sub-agents. | **Accepted (Closed).** Removed env-var bypass; `argv[0]` shims use `fstat` `(st_dev, st_ino)` self-skip + `ACLI_SHIM_DEPTH > 4 -> exit 78`. | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `design.md` D2, `research/01` §1.3 |
| `OPUS-R1-12` | Medium | `--confirm-plan` `plan_hash` treated as authorization for `--no-sandbox`. | **Accepted (Closed).** `plan_hash` is integrity-only; non-yolo `--no-sandbox` requires `/dev/tty` confirmation or unsandboxed-parent `ACLI_ALLOW_UNSANDBOXED_HOST=1`. | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/01` §2 |
| `OPUS-R1-13` | Medium | Mode B HTTP/2 `:authority` binding, coalescing, and upgrades unspecified. | **Accepted (Closed).** Mode B `CONNECT == SNI == (:authority \| Host)` (`421`), Mode A `Host == fqdn` (`421`), upstream `Host`/`:authority` overwrite, `1:1` pools, `0` redirects, `400` on `Upgrade`/`h2c`/CL+TE. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` B3/F5, `research/02` §4 |
| `OPUS-R1-14` | Medium | Linux Landlock ABI floor/fallback unspecified and Landlock TCP rules are port-only. | **Accepted (Closed).** `/usr/bin/bwrap --unshare-all --dev /dev` + userns `overlayfs` + `slirp4netns --disable-host-loopback` + Landlock `ABI >= 1` (`exit 71` if absent). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` D2, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R1-15` | Medium | Missing fail-closed rule when `--mode yolo` auto-VM cannot boot. | **Accepted (Closed).** `--mode yolo` never falls back to host (`exit 64` w/ `--no-sandbox`; `exit 69` preflight; `exit 70` boot/handshake). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` D1/F7, `research/01` §2.2 |
| `OPUS-R1-16` | Medium | Raw guest NATS/HTTP port references contradicted zero-secrets-in-guest invariant. | **Accepted (Closed).** All pane bus traffic uses `vsock:5040` (`acli-bus-rpc/v1`) with NKey seeds held solely in host `aclid`. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `design.md` D3, `research/03` §3–§4 |
| `OPUS-R1-17` | Medium | Untrusted guest PTY bytes reached host terminal emulator unfiltered (`OSC 52`, `DCS`). | **Accepted (Closed).** Allowlist outbound VT filter across `--vm` and host Supervised Launch (`OSC 52` only on host `--allow-osc52`). | `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md`, `design.md` D7, `research/02` §5 |
| `OPUS-R1-18` | Medium | Zone-2 `allow_read_only` search MCPs permitted unbounded secret exfiltration. | **Accepted (Closed).** `<= 512 B` query, `<= 10/min`, `<= 2 MiB/session`, URL-fetch deny in `--mode yolo`, DLP regexes, `--mcp-profile offline`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` B3, `research/02` §4 |
| `OPUS-R1-19` | Medium | `cmd://` `SecretRef` source/path rules unspecified (repo config RCE risk). | **Accepted (Closed).** `cmd://` restricted strictly to `0600` `$HOME/.config/acli/auth.toml` with allowlisted helpers (`op`, `security`, `secret-tool`, `exit 78`). | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `research/01` §4 |
| `OPUS-R1-20` | Medium | Intra-VM resource gaps (`tmpfs` DoS, PID-1 OOM, nested userns, shared `/tmp`). | **Accepted (Closed).** PID-1 `oom_score_adj=-1000` (`1024 MiB` reserve); per-pane `memory.max=1024M`/`1536M`, `memory.oom.group=1`, `pids.max=512`, `256m` `/tmp`, locked `user.max_user_namespaces`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` B2, `research/02` §3 |
| `OPUS-R1-21` | Low | Socket path drift across docs and macOS `104 B` `sun_path` overflow risk. | **Accepted (Closed).** `<ACLI_RUN_DIR>` (`<= 64 B`, `$HOME/.acli/run` on macOS) so `sun_path <= 103 B`. | `design.md` Glossary, `research/README.md` §2–§3 |
| `OPUS-R1-22` | Low | Mode B MITM CA key memory residency and rotation lifecycle underspecified. | **Accepted (Closed).** `mlock`ed `Zeroizing` ECDSA P-256 CA (`RLIMIT_CORE=0`), critical `NameConstraints`, rotated per VM boot and `<= 24 h`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` B3/F5, `research/02` §4 |
| `OPUS-R1-23` | Low | Zone-3 `prompt_on_host` approval UI could be spoofed by inline guest PTY output. | **Accepted (Closed).** Strictly out-of-band via `herdr` protocol-22 modal or `acli approve <req_id>` over `ctl.sock`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` B3, `research/02` §4 |
| `OPUS-R1-24` | Low | Host `git` inspection hardening against `.git/config` `fsmonitor` not normative. | **Accepted (Closed).** Hardened `git rev-parse` (`-c core.fsmonitor=false -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null`) + raw `--get-regexp` / `--includes` B4 hook discovery. | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/01` §1.3 |

### 3.2 `FABLE` Round 1 (`FABLE-R1-01` .. `FABLE-R1-26`)

| ID | Sev | Finding Summary | Disposition & Canonical Remediation | Remediated Artifacts |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `FABLE-R1-01` | Critical | Path A trigger interpolated unsanitized peer bytes into terminal PTYs. | **Accepted (Closed).** Fixed ASCII `[acli-bus] unread=<N> max_id=<M> verified=<true\|false>; run acli msg inbox or bus_inbox` (`0` peer bytes). | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `design.md` D3, `research/03` §5 |
| `FABLE-R1-02` | Critical | Message-level `Nats-Msg-Id` dropped wakeups for recipients after the first. | **Accepted (Closed).** Pane-scoped `Nats-Msg-Id: am:<proj_hash>:<pane_id>:<msg_id>` across `to`/`cc`/`bcc`. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `design.md` D3/F3, `research/03` §3 |
| `FABLE-R1-03` | High | Write-through wakeup lacked a durable outbox and explicit cursor commit rule. | **Accepted (Closed).** `PENDING -> SENT -> PUBLISHED` `outbox.db` + tailer-only contiguous `next_cursor` + post-`PubAck` SQLite recovery. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `design.md` F3, `research/03` §3 |
| `FABLE-R1-04` | High | Edge-triggered wakeup delivery stranded messages arriving during non-idle states. | **Accepted (Closed).** Level-triggered `AckExplicit` queues + `2 s` idle timer + per-path acks. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §5 |
| `FABLE-R1-05` | High | `InjectPrompt` `Enter` keystroke could confirm an unclassified modal dialog. | **Accepted (Closed).** 9-state FSM priority + prompt glyph + `DECSET 2004` + typed `0x07`/`0x08` `written_char_len` cancel + `composer_empty` check. | `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md`, `design.md` D7, `research/02` §5 |
| `FABLE-R1-06` | High | Denylist-based AgentMail proxy missed `macro_*`, `*_product`, and `llm_mode=true`. | **Accepted (Closed).** Normative 9-method `acli-bus-rpc/v1` allowlist (`-32003` on unlisted methods/keys, RFC 8785 JCS `tools/list` pin). | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `design.md` D3, `research/03` §3 |
| `FABLE-R1-07` | High | Contradictory pane transport surface and shared HTTP upstream lost per-pane identity. | **Accepted (Closed).** Uniform `stdio` `acli mcp serve-bus` across all 9 CLIs (`0` HTTP bus routes). | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `design.md` D3, `research/03` §6.1 |
| `FABLE-R1-08` | High | Over-broad pane NATS ACLs allowed forged wakeups, lease writes, and peer reads. | **Accepted (Closed).** Host-held NKey seeds + host-brokered `ACLI_WAKEUPS`, `KV_*`, and `ACLI_OBJ` (`0` pane `$JS.API`/`$JS.ACK`/`KV` rights). | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `design.md`, `research/03` §4 |
| `FABLE-R1-09` | High | Dual build-slot locks across AgentMail, NATS KV, and cgroups could desynchronize. | **Accepted (Closed).** Sole-authority `KV_LEASES_<proj_hash>` (`1800 s` cap, immutable `lease_generation` + `current_revision` CAS). | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `design.md` F4, `research/03` §3 |
| `FABLE-R1-10` | High | `aclid` restart lost RAM-only bindings for surviving host panes; NATS key custody unspecified. | **Accepted (Closed).** HMAC-sealed `panes-registry.json` + Ed25519 re-proof + Keychain / keyring `keys/`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `design.md` F1 |
| `FABLE-R1-11` | Medium | Recycling `tmux` `%N` or PIDs as `pane_id` caused subject/KV/socket collisions. | **Accepted (Closed).** Non-recycling `12`-char CSPRNG `^[a-z0-9]{12}$`. | `design.md` Glossary, `research/README.md` §2 |
| `FABLE-R1-12` | Medium | Path A hardcoded `verified=true` on unverified mail; intra-VM trust boundary note needed. | **Accepted (Closed).** `verified=true` ONLY if all unread messages came via `aclid`; shared-kernel B2 note in `design.md`. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `design.md` B2/Risks, `research/03` §5 |
| `FABLE-R1-13` | Medium | Host file-read mitigation missed Markdown/HTML body image links. | **Accepted (Closed).** Validator-level attachment stripping + `CONVERT_IMAGES=false` + Markdown/HTML image stripping. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §3 |
| `FABLE-R1-14` | Medium | File reservations lacked host-mode enforcement, renewal, and glob normalization. | **Accepted (Closed).** Normalized `globset` jailing, `renew`, hard Diff Gate, and mandatory host `acli git` enforcement (`exit 4` `E4:file_reserved`). | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §3 |
| `FABLE-R1-15` | Medium | `bus_wait` relied on optional `notifications/progress` and lacked cancel/batch bounds. | **Accepted (Closed).** `timeout_ms=25000` (`min=1000` or `0` poll, `max=55000`), `max_messages=20` (`max=50`), cursor acks, non-acking `notifications/cancelled`. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §5 |
| `FABLE-R1-16` | Medium | `acli mcp sync` lacked `.lock`/`.bak`/symlink/AST merge rules and mixed OpenCode shapes. | **Accepted (Closed).** Stat-checked `.lock` + pruned `0600` `.bak` (`<= 3`) AST merge with uniform OpenCode `"type": "local"` `stdio`. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §6.1 |
| `FABLE-R1-17` | Medium | Negated predicates, unknown groups, or empty tool sets could fail open in `AgentGateway` CEL. | **Accepted (Closed).** Positive-only `{require}`/`{allow}` CEL rules per enumerated group (`0` negation, empty sets -> `false`). | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §6.1 |
| `FABLE-R1-18` | Medium | `bus_build_slot` MCP tool could act as an unapproved shell command runner. | **Accepted (Closed).** Lease-only `acquire\|extend\|release`; CLI validates `argv[0]` toolchain allowlist. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §3 & §6.2 |
| `FABLE-R1-19` | Medium | Loopback daemon auth and Linux loopback-deny mechanism were underspecified. | **Accepted (Closed).** JWT on `:4222`, `HTTP_BEARER_TOKEN` on `:8765`, `LocalAPIKey` on `:8090`, and Linux `slirp4netns --disable-host-loopback`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md` |
| `FABLE-R1-20` | Medium | Shared stream quota allowed Object Store spills to evict wakeups; `< 256 B` cap too tight. | **Accepted (Closed).** Dedicated `ACLI_WAKEUPS_<proj_hash>` (`64 MiB`, `< 512 B`), `ACLI_EVENTS_<proj_hash>`, and `ACLI_OBJ_<proj_hash>`. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §4 |
| `FABLE-R1-21` | Medium | Beads Plane 3 lacked a safe writer model across sandbox overlays. | **Accepted (Closed).** `.beads/` RO in panes; allowlisted `acli msg bead` / `bus_bead` (`--actor=<value>`) under `beads-<proj_hash>.lock` on writes. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §3 |
| `FABLE-R1-22` | Medium | `project_key` derivation drifted between docs and collapsed submodules/bare repos. | **Accepted (Closed).** Canonical 4-case `project_key` across all specs and research. | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `research/README.md` §2 |
| `FABLE-R1-23` | Medium | Wall-clock `6000 ms` heartbeat killed VMs on laptop sleep; single pane error dropped `5102`. | **Accepted (Closed).** `CLOCK_MONOTONIC` / `CLOCK_UPTIME_RAW` + `PaneProtocolError{pane_id}` vs bounded VM recycle (`exit 70` `E70:vsock_control_corrupt`). | `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md`, `design.md` D7/F4/F8, `research/02` §5 |
| `FABLE-R1-24` | Low | Cross-document token, socket, verb-count, and error-code drift. | **Accepted (Closed).** Unified `ACLI_BUS_TOKEN`, `<ACLI_RUN_DIR>`, `-32003`, 10-verb `acli msg`, and 9-tool `acli mcp serve-bus`. | `design.md`, `specs/*`, `research/*` |
| `FABLE-R1-25` | Low | Prompt latency conflated RPC enqueue with submit; `0` polling vs host tailer wording. | **Accepted (Closed).** Split `enqueue_latency` (`< 1 ms`) vs post-echo `submit_latency` (`<= 25 ms`) and `0` client polling vs single host tailer. | `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md`, `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md` |
| `FABLE-R1-26` | Low | `tmux paste-buffer` clobbered default buffer; guest terminal queries leaked to host. | **Accepted (Closed).** Relay-serialized `pty.sock` / `0x07` `InjectPrompt` + allowlist outbound VT filter. | `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md`, `research/02` §5 |

### 3.3 `SOL` Round 1 (`SOL-R1-01` .. `SOL-R1-10`)

| ID | Sev | Finding Summary | Disposition & Canonical Remediation | Remediated Artifacts |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `SOL-R1-01` | High | `Formula/acli.rb` lacked `cargo-zigbuild`/musl sysroot and leaked Darwin `RUSTFLAGS`. | **Accepted (Closed).** SHA-256-pinned musl guest `resource`s + `CARGO_TARGET_*_APPLE_DARWIN_RUSTFLAGS`. | `specs/homebrew-aitools-packaging/spec.md`, `design.md` D4, `research/04` §3 |
| `SOL-R1-02` | Medium | `scripts/install-agent-skills.sh` was hardcoded to `ripwire` and 6 CLIs. | **Accepted (Closed).** `--tool ripwire\|agent-mail\|acli\|all` across all 9 CLIs (`0` `ripwire` hard dep). | `proposal.md`, `specs/homebrew-aitools-packaging/spec.md`, `tasks.md` |
| `SOL-R1-03` | Medium | `external-builders = []` does not disable stock Nix remote builders. | **Accepted (Closed).** Cold-store CI gate uses stock Nix `--option builders ''`. | `specs/homebrew-aitools-packaging/spec.md`, `design.md` D6, `research/04` §4 |
| `SOL-R1-04` | Medium | Homebrew signing explanation and DER slot assertion were incomplete. | **Accepted (Closed).** `--preserve-metadata` + `MachO.codesign!`, `__TEXT,__info_plist`, and decoded binary slot 5/7 assertions. | `specs/homebrew-aitools-packaging/spec.md`, `design.md` D4, `research/04` §3 |
| `SOL-R1-05` | High | `AM_INTERFACE_MODE=cli mcp-agent-mail self-update` bypassed an `am`-only wrapper. | **Accepted (Closed).** Wrapped both `bin/am` and `bin/mcp-agent-mail` blocking `self-update\|update` (`exit 2`). | `specs/homebrew-aitools-packaging/spec.md`, `research/04` §2.1 |
| `SOL-R1-06` | Medium | `nats-server -c custom.conf` without `listen:` silently bound `0.0.0.0:4222`. | **Accepted (Closed).** Stage-1 field-independent `libexec/bin/nats-bind-preflight` enforcing `--addr 127.0.0.1` (`exit 78` on duplicate setters). | `specs/homebrew-aitools-packaging/spec.md`, `research/04` §2.2 |
| `SOL-R1-07` | High | `Formula/acli.rb` omitted managed-bus runtime dependencies. | **Accepted (Closed).** `artagon/aitools/{agent-mail,nats-server}` + `on_macos/on_arm` `libkrun` + `on_linux` `bubblewrap`/`libkrun`/`slirp4netns`. | `specs/homebrew-aitools-packaging/spec.md`, `research/04` §3 |
| `SOL-R1-08` | Medium | Collision-only `~5 MiB` EROFS left uncovered `/nix/store` paths case-insensitive on Darwin. | **Accepted (Closed).** Full case-sensitive closure-scoped EROFS image built natively on `pkgsHost`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` D6, `research/02` §3 |
| `SOL-R1-09` | Medium | Version-only `versions/aitools-lock.json` missed same-version SHA-256 drift and `br`. | **Accepted (Closed).** `(package, artifact_role, platform)` lockfile + `ACLIVM01` manifest (`exit 70`). | `specs/homebrew-aitools-packaging/spec.md`, `research/04` §4 |
| `SOL-R1-10` | High | Unconditional `NATS_URL` default in `Formula/nats.rb` overrode saved contexts. | **Accepted (Closed).** Guarded fallback on flags, `NATS_URL`, `NATS_CONTEXT`, and `context.txt`. | `specs/homebrew-aitools-packaging/spec.md`, `research/04` §2.2 |

| ID | Sev | Finding Summary | Disposition & Canonical Remediation | Remediated Artifacts |

| `ASTRA-R1-01` | High | Concurrent `vsock:5101` connections lacked stream-to-pane pairing. | **Accepted (Closed).** Single-use `52 B` `ACLIMUX1` preamble (`<= 2000 ms`) on `vsock:5101`. | `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md`, `design.md` D7, `research/02` §5 |
| `ASTRA-R1-02` | High | `aclid` `SIGKILL` left `acli-shim` VM ownership undefined. | **Accepted (Closed).** `ACLID_LIFELINE_FD` + `vm.lock` triggering guest `syncfs()` and `exit 70`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` F1 |
| `ASTRA-R1-03` | High | Ambient recursion-bypass variable skipped `acli` policy. | **Accepted (Closed).** `fstat` `(st_dev, st_ino)` self-skip + `ACLI_SHIM_DEPTH > 4 -> exit 78`. | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `research/01` §1.3 |
| `ASTRA-R1-04` | High | `tmux` `pane_current_command` claim conflicted with XNU `pbsi_comm`. | **Accepted (Closed).** Exact `#{pane_current_command}` per launch mode/binary + `@acli_provider`. | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `research/01` §1.3 |
| `ASTRA-R1-05` | High | 9-state FSM lacked deterministic collision precedence. | **Accepted (Closed).** Strict 9-state precedence (`exited > hung_turn > ... > idle_at_prompt`). | `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md`, `design.md` Glossary |
| `ASTRA-R1-06` | High | Spec and research disagreed on guest NATS routing. | **Accepted (Closed).** Unified 100% on `vsock:5040` (`acli-bus-rpc/v1`). | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §3 |
| `ASTRA-R1-07` | Medium | Host Seatbelt socket allowlist path differed across docs. | **Accepted (Closed).** Unified on `<ACLI_RUN_DIR>/p/<pane_id>/bus.sock`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/README.md` §3 |
| `ASTRA-R1-08` | Medium | `project_key` formula differed by one parent directory. | **Accepted (Closed).** Unified 4-case `project_key` algorithm everywhere. | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `research/README.md` §2 |
| `ASTRA-R1-09` | High | `--mode yolo` + `--no-sandbox` had contradictory outcomes. | **Accepted (Closed).** Always fails closed with `exit 64` (`E64:flag_denied`). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/01` §2.2 |
| `ASTRA-R1-10` | High | Decoupling inner CLI sandboxes weakened `--mode plan\|read-only` unless outer FS was RO. | **Accepted (Closed).** Outer Seatbelt/Landlock omits `<workspace>`/`--add-dir` from writable roots + `sys_git` `E77:plan_readonly`. | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md` |
| `ASTRA-R1-11` | Medium | Minimum `libkrun` version drifted (`1.10+` vs `1.19.8`). | **Accepted (Closed).** `libkrun >= 1.19.8` (`exit 69` if older). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` F7 |
| `ASTRA-R1-12` | Low | Proxy guard denials mixed JSON-RPC `-32003` and `-32601`. | **Accepted (Closed).** JSON-RPC `-32003` uniformly everywhere. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §3 |
| `ASTRA-R1-13` | Medium | "Zero polling" phrasing conflicted with host external-mail tailer. | **Accepted (Closed).** Zero pane-client polling + single `aclid` host `fetch_inbox_events` tailer. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §3 |
| `ASTRA-R1-14` | High | Multi-file diff promotion lacked atomic rollback/recovery. | **Accepted (Closed).** Single-flight 4-barrier `fsync` `promote-<txid>.json` rollback journal (`design.md` `F6`). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` B4/F6 |
| `ASTRA-R1-15` | Medium | Build-slot acquisition lacked partial-failure rollback order. | **Accepted (Closed).** CAS rollback order across `KV_LEASES`, AgentMail, and cgroups. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §3 |
| `ASTRA-R1-16` | Medium | `herdr` daemon reconnect could leave stale push authority state. | **Accepted (Closed).** `aclid` opens a new `server_epoch` and replays full snapshots (`design.md` `F8`). | `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md`, `design.md` D7/F8 |
| `ASTRA-R1-17` | Medium | Prompt injection latency mixed enqueue with paste-to-Enter delay. | **Accepted (Closed).** Split `enqueue_latency` (`< 1 ms`) vs post-echo `submit_latency` (`<= 25 ms`). | `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md`, `research/02` §5 |
| `ASTRA-R1-18` | Medium | Spec scenarios needed negative/attack coverage. | **Accepted (Closed).** Added negative/attack scenarios across all 5 capability specs. | `specs/*/spec.md`, `tasks.md` |
| `ASTRA-R1-19` | Medium | Stage 1 lockfile parity failed before Stage 2 `Formula/acli.rb` existed. | **Accepted (Closed).** `status: "present" \| "staged"` in `versions/aitools-lock.json`. | `specs/homebrew-aitools-packaging/spec.md`, `research/04` §4 |
| `ASTRA-R1-20` | Medium | Pane auth token env var names disagreed across docs. | **Accepted (Closed).** Single canonical `ACLI_BUS_TOKEN` everywhere. | `design.md`, `specs/*`, `research/*` |
| `ASTRA-R1-21` | Medium | Host runs lacked explicit `null` sensor contract for VM-only fields. | **Accepted (Closed).** Per-mode sensor availability (`null` for VM-only fields) in `meta.json`. | `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md` |
| `ASTRA-R1-22` | High | Wakeup `Nats-Msg-Id` lacked recipient identity. | **Accepted (Closed).** Pane-scoped `am:<proj_hash>:<pane_id>:<msg_id>`. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/03` §3 |

### 3.5 `NEON` Round 1 (`NEON-R1-01` .. `NEON-R1-12`) & 3.6 `CARBON` Round 1 (`CARBON-R1-01` .. `CARBON-R1-10`)

| ID | Sev | Finding Summary | Disposition & Canonical Remediation | Remediated Artifacts |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `NEON-R1-03` | Critical | Intra-VM `seccomp` bypassed via `io_uring`/compat ABIs and unlatched `vsock:5040`/`5102`. | **Accepted (Closed).** `AUDIT_ARCH_*` + `io_uring_*` block + `envp`-only `ACLI_SUPERVISOR_KEY` boot-latched `vsock:5040`/`5102`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md` |
| `NEON-R1-04` | Critical | Mode A/B credential exfiltration via redirects, `gho_`, `?key=`, `:authority != SNI`, and coalescing. | **Accepted (Closed).** RFC 3986 allowlists, host Copilot exchange, `x-goog-api-key`, `0` redirects, `421`, `1:1` pools. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §4 |

| `NEON-R1-07` | Critical | Path A `\x1b[201~\r` bracketed-paste breakout and unfiltered `vsock:5101` escapes. | **Accepted (Closed).** Fixed ASCII Path A template + allowlist outbound VT filter. | `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md`, `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md` |

| `OPUS-R3-03` | High | Resolving `core.hooksPath` via `-c core.hooksPath=/dev/null` always returned `/dev/null`. | **Refined in `OPUS-R4-03`; verified closed in R5.** | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R3-04` | Medium | B4 missed `.husky/**`, `lefthook`, `.pnpmfile.cjs`, `bunfig.toml`, `**/rust-toolchain*`, `.bazelrc`. | **Verified closed in R4.** | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R3-05` | Medium | `isatty(0)\|\|isatty(1)\|\|isatty(2)` routed piped `stdout` (`\| jq`) through the PTY relay. | **Verified closed in R4.** | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `research/01` §1.3 |
| `OPUS-R3-06` | Medium | `8 * 1536M + 1G = 13G > 12G` violated `sum(memory.max) + 1 GiB <= VM RAM`. | **Verified closed in R4.** | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §3 |
| `OPUS-R3-07` | Medium | Denying all `.git` writes left `acli git` undefined. | **Refined in `OPUS-R4-01`; verified closed in R5.** | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R3-08` | Medium | Nested `acli` sub-agent spawn inside a sandboxed pane lacked a defined RPC path. | **Refined in `OPUS-R4-02` (further tightened in `OPUS-R5-05,06,12`).** | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R3-09` | Medium | OverlayFS `redirect_dir` and `metacopy` were not pinned off for the Diff Gate. | **Verified closed in R4.** | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §2.1 & §4 |
| `OPUS-R3-10` | Medium | `exit_origin="child"` cannot be written after zero-wrapper Fast-Exec `execvp`. | **Verified closed in R4.** | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `research/01` §2.2 |
| `OPUS-R3-11` | Medium | Enumerated `$HOME` read-denylist missed shell histories, `~/.m2`, and sibling repos. | **Verified closed in R4.** | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §2.1 |

### 6.1 `OPUS` Round 4 (`OPUS-R4-01` .. `OPUS-R4-17`)

| ID | Sev | Finding Summary | Round 5 Verification & R5 -> R6 Status | Target Artifacts |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `OPUS-R4-01` | Critical | `sys_git` subcommand-only allowlist permitted `--output`, `--no-index`, and `-F`. | **Verified closed in R5** (hardening & mode gate added in `OPUS-R5-11` / `ASTRA-R5-06`). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R4-02` | High | `sys_spawn` lacked monotonic containment and descendant unsafe-root checks. | **Refined in `OPUS-R5-01,05,06,12` (pending R6):** Split unsafe-root predicate + total mode order + `0` `--` args. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R4-03` | High | `git config --file` without `--includes` missed `core.hooksPath` in included files. | **Verified closed in R5** (absent includes covered via `--get-regexp` in `OPUS-R5-03`). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R4-04` | Medium | Non-waiting `"or fork once"` detach lost exit status or contradicted `0` processes. | **Verified closed in R5.** | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `research/01` §1.3 |
| `OPUS-R4-05` | Medium | Launch-mode precedence undefined for `!isatty` runs with Supervised flags. | **Verified closed in R5.** | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `research/01` §1.3 |
| `OPUS-R4-06` | Medium | `!isatty(0) && (isatty(1)\|\|isatty(2))` bypassed outbound VT filtering. | **Refined in `OPUS-R5-07` (pending R6):** Full 8-state `isatty(0..2)` table covering `isatty(0) && !isatty(1)`. | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `research/01` §1.3 |
| `OPUS-R4-07` | Medium | Linux host overlay promotion triggers, crash persistence, and `sys_git` undefined. | **Verified closed in R5** (mid-session freeze/remount detailed in `OPUS-R5-09`). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R4-08` | Medium | Unprivileged `userxattr` and `fuse-overlayfs` markers differed from `0:0` char-devs. | **Verified closed in R5** (backend-specific mount opts in `OPUS-R5-17`). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R4-09` | Medium | Mode B `421` check omitted HTTP/1.1 `Host` header and upstream overwrite. | **Verified closed in R5.** | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §4 |
| `OPUS-R4-10` | Medium | `<ACLI_STATE_DIR>/sandbox/ca-bundle.pem` lay outside `$HOME` read allowlist. | **Verified closed in R5** (moved to immutable `<ACLI_RUN_DIR>/ca/pane-<pane_id>-ca-bundle.pem` `0444` in `OPUS-R5-13`). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/01` §4 |
| `OPUS-R4-11` | Medium | Closed `$HOME` toolchain list omitted `T0` realpaths and `~/.bun`, `~/.pyenv`, `mise`. | **Verified closed in R5.** | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R4-12` | Low | B4 omitted executable wrapper scripts (`gradlew`, `mvnw`, `.yarn/releases/**`, `tools/bazel`). | **Verified closed in R5.** | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `OPUS-R4-13` | Low | `TIOCNOTTY` detach must precede `sandbox_init` and close `/dev/tty`. | **Refined in `OPUS-R5-08` (pending R6):** Restore `SIGHUP` to `SIG_DFL` immediately after `TIOCNOTTY`. | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `research/01` §1.3 |
| `OPUS-R4-14` | Low | VM RAM sizing had a gap on `17–23 GiB` hosts and 8-pane mux tests. | **Verified closed in R5** (`host_ram_gib - 4` headroom cap added in `OPUS-R5-15`). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `research/02` §3 |
| `OPUS-R4-15` | Low | `--allow-osc52` opt-out lacked `--mode yolo` / `sys_spawn` rejection rules. | **Verified closed in R5.** | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `research/01`–`02` |
| `OPUS-R4-16` | Low | Overloaded exit codes `4`, `64`, and `78` needed machine-parseable subreasons. | **Verified closed in R5.** | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md`, `research/01` §2.2 |

| `ASTRA-R6-03` | High | `fchmod(0755\|0644)` widened private `0600`/`0640`/`0700` host files during Diff-Promotion. | **Refined in `ASTRA-R7-05` (pending R8):** Preserve `existing_host_st_mode & 0777` verbatim on content-only edits (`0740` stays `0740`, `0744` stays `0744`). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `tasks.md`, `research/02` §4 |
| `ASTRA-R6-04` | Medium | A second crash after writing `ABORTED` before clearing `pending_tx` hit `E70:recovery_corrupt` without an `ABORTED` transition. | **Verified closed in R7.** | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md` F6, `research/02` §4 |
| `ASTRA-R6-05` | High | Schema-drift fallback to `fetch_inbox` could not paginate `> 100` tied timestamps and mutated read state. | **Refined in `ASTRA-R7-01` (pending R8):** Use exact upstream `after=<persisted_cursor>` per `(project_key, agent_name)` + `oldest_available_cursor` level wakeup. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `design.md`, `research/03` §3 |

| `ASTRA-R6-07` | High | When `pending_tx == null`, a replacement broker with a distinct `ACLI_PERSIST_DIR` could not locate the prior owner's `panes-registry.json`. | **Refined in `ASTRA-R7-06` (pending R8):** Hold `flock` on immutable `project-<proj_hash>.lock` and serialize all `.owner` writes under one per-project `.owner` state mutex. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md`, `research/02`–`03` |
| `ASTRA-R6-08` | Medium | Failed post-paste `composer_empty` reset left buffered human input without a safe quarantine rule. | **Refined in `ASTRA-R7-07` (pending R8):** Replay held bytes ONLY after `composer_empty == true` is verified (with double-`Ctrl-C` discard). | `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md`, `tasks.md`, `research/02` §5 |
| `ASTRA-R6-09` | Medium | Claiming `--add-dir` in `writable-scopes.json` during `--mode plan`/`read-only` blocked concurrent read-only readers. | **Verified closed in R7.** | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `research/02`–`03` |

| `ASTRA-R7-02` | High | Broker-allocated `<ACLI_STATE_DIR>/tmp/pane-<pane_id>` failed unsafe-root validation if checked under the caller `TMPDIR` descendant rule. | **Verified closed in R8.** | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `design.md`, `tasks.md`, `research/02` §2.1 |
| `ASTRA-R7-03` | High | Reassigning a retired `AdjectiveNoun` mail name after `7 d` exposed the prior pane's read mail and thread history. | **Refined in `ASTRA-R8-01,04,07`; verified closed in R9** (`mail_epoch` & append-only grant follow-ups tracked in `ASTRA-R9-01,03`). | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `design.md`, `tasks.md`, `research/03` §2–§3 |

### 10.1 `OPUS` Round 8 (`OPUS-R8-01` .. `OPUS-R8-09` — Frozen `APPROVE_WITH_NITS` Implementation Guardrails)

| ID | Sev | Finding Summary | Disposition & Complete Implementation Guardrail (`tasks.md` Cross-Referenced) | Target Artifacts |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `OPUS-R8-01` | Medium | Re-promotion over retained `upper` whiteouts/opaque dirs needs recorded `upper`-side marker state so unchanged retained entries are skipped on subsequent `sys_git` calls. | **Tracked implementation guardrail (`0` Critical/High; cross-referenced in `tasks.md` 3.1/4.5).** Implementation records `upper`-side `(size, blake3, marker_kind)` per promoted entry, skips unchanged retained entries on subsequent promotions (including when `bus_reserve` is held so stale retained copies never overwrite host edits), matches retained opaque dirs by equality-or-descendant, and tests host/peer file creation under a retained whiteout or opaque dir. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md` (`Single-Flight Four-Barrier fchmod/fsync Diff-Promotion Gate and Three-Marker Recovery`), `tasks.md` |
| `OPUS-R8-02` | Medium | `sys_git commit` 8-key config carve-out should reject `gpg.program`/`gpg.ssh.program` or config origin files residing inside pane-writable roots. | **Tracked implementation guardrail (`0` Critical/High; cross-referenced in `tasks.md` 3.2/4.5).** Implementation inspects `git config --show-origin` and rejects (`E77:protected_path`) any identity/signing value whose origin file or program `realpath` falls inside `<workspace>`, `--add-dir`, or `TMPDIR`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md` (`Closed Per-Subcommand sys_git Allowlist with Plan Write Gate and Monotonic sys_spawn`), `tasks.md` |
| `OPUS-R8-03` | Low | Mode `(a)` child `setsid()` + `ioctl(TIOCSCTTY)` must execute prior to `sandbox_init_with_parameters()` (or be in the SBPL ioctl allowlist). | **Tracked implementation guardrail (`0` Critical/High; cross-referenced in `tasks.md` 3.1).** Implementation calls `setsid()` + `ioctl(TIOCSCTTY)` on `PANE_SLAVE_TTY` before `sandbox_init_with_parameters()` (and may additionally include `TIOCSCTTY` on `(literal (param "PANE_SLAVE_TTY"))`). | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md` (`Three-Binary Privilege Separation and Complete Eight-State Supervised vs Fast-Exec Launch`), `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `tasks.md` |
| `OPUS-R8-04` | Low | `ACLIIDX1` bypass-flag matching after `--` should define a `bypass_flags` schema field and canonicalize `--k=v`, short clusters, and camelCase aliases. | **Tracked implementation guardrail (`0` Critical/High; cross-referenced in `tasks.md` 2.1/2.4).** `ACLIIDX1` schema defines per-provider `bypass_flags` (covering approval-mode values `yolo`, `never`, `bypassPermissions`), canonicalizing `--k=v`, short option clusters, and yargs camelCase aliases prior to matching. | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md` (`Canonical Nine-CLI Flag and Mode Compiler`), `tasks.md` |
| `OPUS-R8-05` | Low | Caller host `TMPDIR=$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR` on Linux triggers the `<ACLI_RUN_DIR>` ancestor check (`exit 64` fail-closed). | **Tracked implementation guardrail (`0` Critical/High; cross-referenced in `tasks.md` 3.1).** Fail-closed behavior documented; implementation scopes caller `TMPDIR` vs `<ACLI_RUN_DIR>` to descendant overlap because the child receives private per-pane `TMPDIR`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md` (`Global Default Host OS Kernel Sandbox, Linux Supervised Overlay B4 Gate, and Split Unsafe-Root Checks`), `tasks.md` |
| `OPUS-R8-06` | Low | Minimal Linux containers lacking `/etc/machine-id` need a fallback seed for `$HOME/.acli/run/<hash>`. | **Tracked implementation guardrail (`0` Critical/High; cross-referenced in `tasks.md` 3.1).** Implementation falls back `/etc/machine-id` -> `/var/lib/dbus/machine-id` -> `blake3_16(hostname \|\| boot_id)` (`exit 69` if none is readable). | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md` (`Global Default Host OS Kernel Sandbox, Linux Supervised Overlay B4 Gate, and Split Unsafe-Root Checks`), `tasks.md` |
| `OPUS-R8-07` | Low | `sys_git commit` identity read should cover `gpg.openpgp.program`, `gpg.x509.program`, `gpg.ssh.defaultKeyCommand`, and `author.*`/`committer.*`, failing closed on `git config` exit `!= 0 && != 1`. | **Tracked implementation guardrail (`0` Critical/High; cross-referenced in `tasks.md` 3.2).** Implementation includes `gpg.openpgp.program`, `gpg.x509.program`, `gpg.ssh.defaultKeyCommand`, and `author.*`/`committer.*` keys, treating `exit 1` as absent key and any `exit != 0 && != 1` as fail-closed `E77:protected_path`. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md` (`Closed Per-Subcommand sys_git Allowlist with Plan Write Gate and Monotonic sys_spawn`), `tasks.md` |
| `OPUS-R8-08` | Low | `stash push` and `switch` over pane-edited paths deterministically hit `E4:overlay_dirty` while `upper` is retained until pane exit. | **Tracked implementation guardrail (`0` Critical/High; cross-referenced in `tasks.md` 3.1/3.2).** Fail-closed behavior holds by design until pane exit. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md` (`Closed Per-Subcommand sys_git Allowlist with Plan Write Gate and Monotonic sys_spawn`), `tasks.md` |
| `OPUS-R8-09` | Low | Minor wording notes on `design.md` `blake3` baseline mention, bare `E66` vs `E66:fd_arg_unsupported`, and path-valued `/dev/fd/N` scope. | **Verified closed in R11 (`OPUS-R10-03` / `OPUS-R11-05`; cross-referenced in `tasks.md` 2.1).** `design.md` notes `fstat` + `blake3`; missing-file/cwd exit `66` emits bare `E66` while undeclared normalized `/dev/fd/N` or `/proc/{self,thread-self,<pid>}/fd/N` CLI flags emit `E66:fd_arg_unsupported` (`--pass-fd <N>`). | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md` (`Three-Binary Privilege Separation and Complete Eight-State Supervised vs Fast-Exec Launch`, `Deterministic Exit-Code Taxonomy, Closed Canonical Subreasons, and Scoped Origin Markers`), `design.md`, `tasks.md` |

| `FABLE-R8-07` | Low | Operational notes on HMAC-failure reap PID source (`.owner` `live_panes`), transient Keychain lock retry, `host-ovl` retention, and `.owner` recovery. | **Tracked implementation guardrail (`0` Critical/High; cross-referenced in `tasks.md` 4.1).** Implementation reads `.owner` `live_panes` on HMAC failure, preserves `host-ovl/<pane_id>/`, and retries transient Keychain lock errors before reaping. | `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md`, `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md`, `tasks.md` |
| `FABLE-R8-08` | Low | `/dev/fd/N` and `/proc/self/fd/N` CLI path argument handling relative to internal descriptor allocation. | **Verified closed in R11 (`OPUS-R10-03` -> `OPUS-R11-05`):** Repeatable `--pass-fd <N>` (`N >= 3`), `acli` internal descriptors (`qwen --json-fd <internal_fd>`) strictly above `max(2, max_declared_caller_fd)`, and normalized `/dev/fd/N` & `/proc/{self,thread-self,<pid>}/fd/N` rejection (`E66:fd_arg_unsupported`). | `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md` (`Three-Binary Privilege Separation and Complete Eight-State Supervised vs Fast-Exec Launch`), `tasks.md`, `research/01` §1.3 |

| `ASTRA-R17-04` | Medium | Transitioning `QUEUED_LOCAL -> IN_FLIGHT_SUBMITTED` at hour `47` left participant grant `expires_at = created_at + 48 h`, causing restore filtering (`now < expires_at`) at hour `49` to drop an unresolved `< 7 d` submission's grants. | Inside the same `outbox.db` `BEGIN IMMEDIATE ... COMMIT` transaction that sets `state = 'IN_FLIGHT_SUBMITTED', submitted_at = now`, atomically updated `expires_at = submitted_at + 7 d` on all participant `PROVISIONAL` grants (`WHERE ref_id = 'outbox:' \|\| row_id`), and added an hour-`47` submission / hour-`49` restore test in Task 6.2 (verified closed R18). | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md` (`Valid AdjectiveNoun Identity, Single-DB outbox.db.acli_store_epoch, Mail-Epoch Quarantine, and Proof Custody`, `Brokered Allowlist-Only Nine-Tool acli-bus-rpc/v1 Proxy and Complete Nine-Method Rate Buckets`, `Decoupled Outbox PUBLISHED vs Per-Recipient Tailer next_cursor and Table-Scoped SQLite Pin`, `Normative NATS Subject/Stream/KV/Object-Store Allowlists and Host Key Custody`), `design.md`, `tasks.md`, `research/03` §3 |

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `FABLE-R18-01` | Medium | If an operator restored `storage.sqlite3` in place after `SIGKILL` without `restore-prepare` and ran offline `acli bus wal-retry` before `aclid` started, offline `wal-retry` (`needs_epoch_cas == 0`) could write witness columns and checkpoint the stale crash WAL without running step-1 restore evaluation. | Required offline `acli bus wal-retry` under `instance.lock` to run the startup writer reap, read-only `fcntl(F_GETLK)` writer probe, and step-1 restore evaluation **before** any witness write, checkpoint, or `quick_check(1)` — committing `needs_epoch_cas = 1` and exiting non-zero if a restore is detected (+ Task 6.2 test). | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md` (`Valid AdjectiveNoun Identity, Single-DB outbox.db.acli_store_epoch, Mail-Epoch Quarantine, and Proof Custody`), `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md` (`Volatile Runtime Root, ACLI_INSTANCE, Canonical Per-User Workspace/Scope Lock, and Recovery`), `design.md`, `tasks.md`, `research/03` §2 |
| `FABLE-R18-02` | Low | Continuation to steps (3)–(4) after a `ctl.sock` `wal-retry` pass and the scope of offline `wal-retry` vs the `5 s` startup `quick_check(1)` default were unstated. | Specified that `ctl.sock` `wal-retry` continues directly through steps (3)–(4) clearing `mail_store_degraded = false` without restarting into the `5 s` default, while offline `wal-retry` under `instance.lock` clears a crash checkpoint without deleting the WAL or bumping `mail_epoch`. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md` (`Valid AdjectiveNoun Identity, Single-DB outbox.db.acli_store_epoch, Mail-Epoch Quarantine, and Proof Custody`), `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md` (`Volatile Runtime Root, ACLI_INSTANCE, Canonical Per-User Workspace/Scope Lock, and Recovery`), `research/03` §2 |
| `FABLE-R18-03` | Low | `restore-prepare --from <backup_db>` did not require committing `needs_epoch_cas = 1` first, differed on `-wal`/`-shm` move order, did not state `ENOENT` tolerance when prior `storage.sqlite3` is absent, and left `'expects an existing store'` implicit. | Committed `needs_epoch_cas = 1` first before any file step in `restore-prepare --from`, unified sidecar move order (`linkat` prior DB if present — tolerating `ENOENT` when recovering a missing DB — BEFORE `renameat`ing temp backup, then moving `-wal`/`-shm` into quarantine immediately after `renameat`), and defined `'expects an existing store'` as `db_ino != 0 \|\| mail_epoch > 0`. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md` (`Valid AdjectiveNoun Identity, Single-DB outbox.db.acli_store_epoch, Mail-Epoch Quarantine, and Proof Custody`), `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md` (`Volatile Runtime Root, ACLI_INSTANCE, Canonical Per-User Workspace/Scope Lock, and Recovery`), `design.md`, `research/03` §2 |
| `FABLE-R18-04` | Low | Quarantine entry component formats (`<utc_ts>`, lowercase `<store_gen>`, 8-hex `<rand8>`), exclusive `mkdirat` for stale-WAL entries, offline `clean_shutdown == 0` refusal consequence, and manual full-disk override were unstated. | Defined `<utc_ts>` (`YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ`), lowercase UUIDv4 `<store_gen>`, and 8-char lowercase hex `<rand8>` next to the regex, used exclusive `mkdirat` for both quarantine entry types, stated that offline `quarantine-prune -- crash-copy` always refuses when `clean_shutdown == 0`, and noted manual offline removal as the full-disk break-glass override. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md` (`Valid AdjectiveNoun Identity, Single-DB outbox.db.acli_store_epoch, Mail-Epoch Quarantine, and Proof Custody`), `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md` (`Volatile Runtime Root, ACLI_INSTANCE, Canonical Per-User Workspace/Scope Lock, and Recovery`), `specs/acli-launcher-and-metadata/spec.md` (`Three-Binary Privilege Separation and Complete Eight-State Supervised vs Fast-Exec Launch`), `design.md`, `tasks.md`, `research/03` §2 |
| `FABLE-R18-05` | Low | In `MUX:61`, while `path_a_quarantined == true && orphan_paste_open == true`, an embedded `0x03` byte in late paste data (`Ground`) could match rule (1) and forward `0x03` once to the child PTY. | Applied the same standalone 1-byte read bounded by `>= 50 ms` `stdin` idle before and after check to rule (1) whenever `orphan_paste_open == true`. | `specs/acli-mux-herdr-ntm/spec.md` (`Typed Ordered InjectPrompt with Provider Reset, Verified composer_empty, and tmux/ntm Identity`), `design.md`, `tasks.md`, `research/02` §5, `research/03` §5 |
| `FABLE-R18-06` | Low | When a local `bd`/`br` mutation exited `0` and only the `<= 4 s` JSONL export timed out, `BUS:16` risked returning `bead_subprocess_timeout` for a committed mutation; killed mutations omitted `intent_id`, and `LOCK_SH` polling lacked writer priority. | Returned mutation success with `"reconcile_pending": true` + host `Attention` when the mutation exits `0` and only the `<= 4 s` export fails/times out, included `intent_id` in `-32003` `bead_subprocess_timeout` (`"outcome_unknown": true`), gated new `bd` readers behind an in-process writer-pending flag, and termed the `5 s` `bd` reader bound a lock-wait budget. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md` (`Three-Plane Coordination, Explicit ACLI_INSTANCE Isolation, and Canonical Per-User Lock`), `design.md`, `tasks.md`, `research/03` §3 |
| `FABLE-R18-07` | Low | Minor cross-file phrasing items (`BUS:25` & `clean_shutdown = 1` `needs_epoch_cas == 0` gate, offline `restore-prepare`/`wal-retry` `fcntl(F_GETLK)` byte-range lock probe, `quick_check(1)` in `DES`/`TASKS` offline heartbeat, and `512 MiB` copy-only floor wording). | Added `needs_epoch_cas == 0` to `BUS:25` and `clean_shutdown = 1` witness writes, named `-shm` `WAL_WRITE_LOCK` (byte `120`) and `storage.sqlite3` `RESERVED`/`EXCLUSIVE` (`0x40000001..0x40000002`) `fcntl(F_GETLK)` active-write-transaction probes across `BUS:24` and `research/03` §2, and aligned `OSS:139`, `design.md`, and `tasks.md`. | `specs/interagent-messaging-bus/spec.md` (`Valid AdjectiveNoun Identity, Single-DB outbox.db.acli_store_epoch, Mail-Epoch Quarantine, and Proof Custody`), `specs/acli-os-and-vm-sandboxing/spec.md` (`Volatile Runtime Root, ACLI_INSTANCE, Canonical Per-User Workspace/Scope Lock, and Recovery`), `design.md`, `tasks.md`, `research/03` §2 |
